- **schliff**: Deterministic linter and scorer for instruction files — SKILL.md, AGENTS.md, CLAUDE.md. No model in the loop: the same bytes score the same everywhere. Use for linting, scoring, auditing or CI-gating an instruction file, and for checking whether the commands a file promises actually resolve in the repo.
